SELECT `c0`.`Key` AS `Name`, (
    SELECT `c1`.`courier_id`
    FROM (
        SELECT `c2`.`courier_id`, LOWER(`c2`.`courier_service_name`) AS `Key`
        FROM `courier_service` AS `c2`
    ) AS `c1`
    WHERE (`c0`.`Key` = `c1`.`Key`) OR (`c0`.`Key` IS NULL AND (`c1`.`Key` IS NULL))
    LIMIT 1) AS `CourierId`
FROM (
    SELECT LOWER(`c`.`courier_service_name`) AS `Key`
    FROM `courier_service` AS `c`
) AS `c0`
